| `test_metrics_401_in_live_mode_without_a_key_or_token` | Live: no key or a wrong token 401, an API key 403; the metrics token, a dashboard key and an admin key 200 with key names in the labels; the token reads nothing else; without reader keys the token alone opens it, and without the token too it is 403 |
| `test_metrics_token_is_checked_at_startup` | A short token, or one that is also an API, admin or dashboard key, stops startup |
| `test_public_live_metrics_scrub_client_labels` | A public live dashboard: anonymous `/metrics` and request lookups show `client-2`, never a client name; a keyed reader sees `globex` |
| `test_scrape_time_gauges` | Version traffic for the live and canary versions, the student breaker open after a test run outage and the teacher's closed, today's spend |
| `test_metrics_module_format` | Label and help escaping, a counter refusing to go down or take the wrong label count, duplicate names refused, bad buckets refused, `Gauge.replace` dropping old series, NaN observations ignored, cumulative buckets, `number()` formatting, relabelled series added together, `client_label` |
| `test_a_failure_never_starts_the_live_ledgers_day_one` | On a fresh live (`auto_day`) file a 401 leaves the origin unset (day 0); day 1 starts at the first request 20 h later; a later failure is filed under day 2; after reopening the file the origin is the same and the next request is still day 1 |
| `test_old_ledger_gains_request_ids_and_the_failures_table` | An old file gains `request_id`, `client_request_id` and the `failures` table; the lookup uses `requests_request_id`; the stage list; `spend_since` adds answered and failed spend per client since a time |

### `tests/test_watch.py`

The live gateway watchdog (FR-11.32 to FR-11.34, NFR-66, [ADR 0132](adr/0132-demo-watchdog-and-rollback-first-updates.md)), offline through the seams of `ratchet.watch.Env` (`fetch`, `run`, `clock`, `sleep`, `out`): the module's import without site-packages; the settings and their checks; the signed alert, its retries and de-duplication; the classification table (a 502 with `upstream 402` is a different code from the same 502 with `upstream 503`); the restart rule on a fake `docker` (a teacher outage and a busy ledger never restart; a hung gateway and a broken ledger do; the grace, cooldown, hourly cap, pause and update lock; a stopped container is never started); the probe (the pinned-task preflight, the request's headers and size, retry once, the flag, no leak of a key or an upstream message); `flag_view`, `Runtime.live_check` and `inside flag|inspect` on a real ledger; `scripts/demo_check.py`; the cron file and cloud-init; and `update.sh`, `backup.sh` and `restore.sh` run with a fake `docker` and `sleep` first on `PATH` (the dry run, the backup-first order, the rollback, the restore only when the old image cannot open the ledger, no rollback for a teacher outage, a refused config check).

### `tests/test_readiness.py`

The startup configuration check, the config check CLI, liveness and readiness (FR-12.30, NFR-35, NFR-2, INV-1; [ADR 0070](adr/0070-configuration-checks-and-readiness.md)). Live mode runs offline through `Runtime(transports=)`, `FakeOpenAIServer` and `httpx.MockTransport`; every test clears the shell's `RATCHET_*` variables first.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_bad_setting_stops_startup_naming_the_variable` (29 cases) | Each bad value (an unparsable or out-of-range number, a bad mode, a non-http(s) URL, an empty Token Factory key, no API keys, a short admin key, release policy rules across fields, an unwritable or missing `RATCHET_DB`, a bad timeout, an unknown teacher failure mode, a bad 0/1 switch, a closed or unknown teacher, judge or fallback teacher) stops a live `Runtime` with a `ConfigError` (a `ValueError`) naming the variable, before the ledger file exists; with the variable fixed it starts |
| `test_every_error_is_listed_at_once` | Six bad variables give one error listing all six (the unparsable one first, as `load()` read it); a refused teacher makes it a `LicenseError` too; outside `load()` a bad variable still raises at once with `var`, `value` and `expected`; `load()` keeps the default and collects the issue |
| `test_readers_that_check_several_variables_report_each_one` | Sixteen bad variables across the release policy, the cost and generation policies, the logs, a breaker and the reserved settings, each reader of which stops at its first problem: one `ConfigError` names all sixteen; `ReleasePolicy.env_issues` alone; `config.defaults()` ignores the environment |
| `test_config_check_and_startup_agree_on_every_variable` | In mock and live mode, every variable Ratchet reads set to `garbage` and to `-1`: `python -m ratchet.config check` exits 2 exactly when `Runtime` refuses to start, and a refusal is a `ConfigError` before the ledger file exists (a bad `RATCHET_TASKS_FILE` is the loader's `SpecError`) |
| `test_misspelled_variable_warns_with_a_suggestion_and_strict_mode_stops` | `RATCHET_AUDIT_RAT` warns with "did you mean RATCHET_AUDIT_RATE?", an unrelated name without a suggestion, the VM's own variables not at all; the warning is kept and logged without the value; `RATCHET_STRICT_CONFIG=1` makes it fatal, a bad strict value is an error, strict and clean starts |
| `test_warnings_for_settings_that_do_nothing` | A mock-only variable in live mode (not at its default), a 0/1 switch spelled otherwise, a student endpoint without a token, a live audit rate under 1%, an unpriced model, an empty key for a teacher off Nebius, an unpriced hosted fallback student: warnings, never errors, none in mock mode |
| `test_release_policy_rules_across_fields` | `ReleasePolicy.validate`: the default and the edges are clean; a floor above the bar, 0 audits, 0 shadow answers and a tolerance over 0.2 are errors naming their variable, all four listed together; such policies can still be built |
| `test_config_check_cli_exit_codes` | `python -m ratchet.config check`: 0 clean, 1 warnings, 2 errors (errors listed first, counts in the last line), 2 for warnings under strict; `--json`; usage errors; in its own process in live mode it reports every error and creates no ledger file |
| `test_config_check_cli_loads_the_tasks_file` | The CLI reports a bad `RATCHET_TASKS_FILE` (every spec error), accepts a good one, refuses `clients` that name no API key, and reports a missing file |
| `test_known_env_covers_every_variable_the_deploy_kit_sets` | Every `RATCHET_*` name in the deploy kit, the Dockerfiles, the Makefile, the scripts and the simulator is known, so the VM's `.env` never warns; mock-only and loose switches are in `KNOWN_ENV`; `DEPLOY_ENV` does not overlap it |
| `test_update_script_checks_the_config_and_waits_for_readyz` | `deploy/vm/update.sh` runs the check before `docker compose up -d` and waits for `/readyz` after it; the Docker health check stays on `/healthz` |
| `test_healthz_answers_with_every_worker_thread_busy` | `/healthz`, `/livez` and `/readyz` are coroutines and answer while AnyIO's thread limiter is fully borrowed |
| `test_readyz_in_mock_mode_checks_the_ledger_only` | Mock mode: `ledger` and `config` (with the warning's message) only, the flags, `Cache-Control: no-store` |
| `test_readyz_503_when_the_ledger_cannot_be_written` | A read-only connection and a closed file ledger are 503 with `read_only` and `closed`; a lock held past the probe's wait is 200 `degraded` (`lock_timeout`, `busy_runs` 1) and 503 when the next probe still finds it held, `ready` once released; a probe that outlasts its caller (`timeout`) counts as busy too; results are cached for 15 s on the runtime's clock; no failures rows |
| `test_readyz_reports_missing_adapters_and_queues_their_reload` | Teacher `/models` 200 and a student `/models` without the canary's `student-v3`: 200 `degraded`, `missing_adapters=['student-v3']`, the reload sent to the endpoint; each key sent to its own endpoint; no URL or key in the answer; one probe per TTL, the student's failed result for 15 s; `ready` once the adapter is listed; an ok student result reused for 5 minutes |
| `test_readyz_retries_a_timed_out_teacher_check_once` | FR-12.30, NFR-2: a first `/models` call that times out is tried once more; a second answer is `ready`, two timeouts are `degraded` (`ConnectTimeout`), a refused connection is not retried, and the teacher's wait stays under 5 s |
| `test_readyz_follows_the_teacher` | An open teacher breaker is 503, unless a fallback teacher or a standing-in student keeps it 200 `degraded`; a 401 model list is 503; a 500, an unreadable list, a list without the teacher model and a connection error are `degraded` (the error named by class, no URL); an open student breaker is `degraded`; without a student endpoint the hosted fallback is reported |
| `test_readyz_recovers_from_an_open_teacher_breaker_without_traffic` | An open teacher breaker is 503 while it waits; past its wait, with no request at all for up to a day, 200 `degraded` with `probe_due` while `/models` answers, 503 while the endpoint is unreachable; half-open is `degraded`; a failed probe is 503 for the doubled wait, a successful one `ready`; a fallback teacher's due probe counts on its own endpoint, and on the teacher's only when the endpoint answers |
| `test_hung_endpoints_never_make_the_ledger_look_unwritable` | Teacher and student `/models` that hang hold both HTTP probe threads; the ledger's own thread still answers, 200 `degraded`, also once its result is stale; the hung runs are shared, not started again |
| `test_probe_cache_shares_runs_times_out_and_names_classes` | `ProbeCache`: one run per name per TTL shared by concurrent callers, a caller's timeout that leaves the run going (and keeps its result), a raising probe named by class only, `clear()` |
| `test_ledger_error_codes_and_model_lists` | `ledger_error`'s codes; `list_models` reads an OpenAI model list, sends no `Authorization` without a key and calls a `data` that is not a list unreadable |

### `tests/test_idempotency.py`

Retry de-duplication (FR-1.70, INV-3, NFR-6; [ADR 0071](adr/0071-idempotency-keys-and-retry-de-duplication.md)): `Idempotency-Key` answers, the SDK's retry count and the ledger's seen window. The gateway tests run the mock gateway with a `RecordingProvider` teacher (cold start: the teacher answers and the example is stored); TTLs and windows run on a `FakeClock` (`Runtime(clock=)`); threads meet at known points by polling `IdemCache.stats()["waiting"]` or a `BlockingTeacher`'s events, never by sleeping for a guess. Metrics are process-wide, so the tests compare deltas.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_keys_retry_counts_and_fingerprints_parse_strictly` | `check_key` (1 to 255 printable ASCII, trimmed), `retry_count` (a plain count, else 0), `fingerprint` (key order ignored; the task header and the body count) |
| `test_idem_cache_keeps_expires_evicts_and_releases` | Replays, `KeyReused`, keys per client, a second `finish` ignored, the TTL on the cache's clock, a failure releasing the key, least-recently-used eviction that never drops a running entry, the byte budget (an oversized answer not kept), the sweep, bad arguments, `enabled` |
| `test_idem_cache_byte_budget_counts_each_entry_not_only_its_body` | Each kept answer counts its body plus `ENTRY_OVERHEAD` (2 KiB), so tiny answers stop at the byte budget whatever `max_entries` allows; an answer whose body fits but whose entry does not is not kept |
| `test_idem_cache_waiters_share_the_answer_or_take_over_after_a_failure` | A waiter gets the owner's answer even with TTL 0; another body refused while the first runs; after a failure one waiter takes over and the other gets its answer; a wait that runs out is `InFlight` |
| `test_waiters_per_key_are_bounded` | Past `max_waiters` (default `MAX_WAITERS_PER_KEY`, 4) a duplicate is `InFlight` at once; another key is not affected; the slots are given back |
| `test_seen_window_counts_once_per_window_per_sender` | `SeenWindow`: once per window per sender, a skipped repeat not restarting it, the oldest pair forgotten first |
| `test_add_example_skips_retries_and_repeats_within_the_window` | `Ledger.add_example(count_seen=, sender=)`: no window counts every repeat; `count_seen=False` never counts and closes the refused INSERT's transaction; the window per sender ('' included), internal traffic (None) always counting, a repeat after the window, a new example starting its window; `ratchet_seen_skipped_total` |
| `test_concurrent_retries_count_once` | Eight threads repeating one example for one client count once; eight clients count once each |
| `test_old_ledger_gains_retry_count` | An old file gains `requests.retry_count` (old rows 0) |
| `test_same_idempotency_key_replays_without_a_cascade` | One ledger row and one teacher call; the replay's body byte-identical (same completion id), `Idempotent-Replayed: true`, the answer's headers but no rate-limit headers, its own `x-request-id`; served after the daily quota is used up while a new key gets 429; another client's key space; outcome counts |
| `test_concurrent_duplicates_call_the_teacher_once` | A duplicate that arrives while the teacher is blocked waits and gets the same answer (`joined`); one teacher call, one row |
| `test_key_reused_with_another_body_or_malformed_is_a_400` | Another body or task header: 400 `idempotency_key_reused` with `X-Should-Retry: false`; malformed keys 400 `invalid_idempotency_key`; bad JSON never claims its key; `ratchet_rejections_total{reason="idempotency"}` |
| `test_a_body_too_deep_to_fingerprint_is_a_400_and_claims_nothing` | Regression: a fingerprint that hits `RecursionError` is a 400 `invalid_json`, not a 500, with no key claimed, no teacher call and no failures row; around the real nesting limit (found by bisection without a key) every request with a key is a 400 |
| `test_in_flight_wait_is_bounded_by_the_teacher_deadline` | After `RATCHET_TEACHER_DEADLINE_S` the duplicate gets 409 `idempotency_key_in_use` with `Retry-After: 1`; once the first finished, the key replays |
| `test_idempotency_ttl_expiry_runs_again` | On a fake clock: a replay at 599 s, a second cascade at 601 s |
| `test_streamed_answer_is_replayed_as_the_same_events` | A stream with `include_usage` replayed as the same events and headers; the same key without `stream` is reused |
| `test_failed_first_request_releases_the_key` | A 502 is never kept: the retry with the key runs and answers |
| `test_idempotency_off_ignores_the_header` | `RATCHET_IDEMPOTENCY_CACHE=0`: every request runs and the header is not checked |
| `test_replay_log_line_says_it_replayed` | The chat log line's `retry_count` and `idempotent_replay`; the replay's `replay_of` names the request that ran, its `cost_usd` is 0; the key never logged |
| `test_sdk_retries_do_not_drain_the_golden_set` | Three escalated retries with `x-stainless-retry-count: 1` leave a golden example at `seen` 1 and in `dataset.rows(ledger, "golden")`, rows recording the count; a keyless client's repeats within 300 s count once; another client's counts |
| `test_the_same_request_minutes_later_still_counts` | Repeats ten minutes apart (fake clock) count, and the third moves the example to training (`FREQUENT`) |
| `test_openai_sdk_retry_after_a_server_error_is_not_a_repeat` | The real OpenAI SDK's retry of a request that failed after its example was stored sends `x-stainless-retry-count: 1`, and `seen` stays 1 |
| `test_internal_traffic_and_mock_mode_count_every_repeat` | Mock mode leaves the window off by default; calls outside a gateway request (no meta, or a meta whose request is not in flight) count every repeat; an explicit retry count is still honoured |
| `test_retry_settings_are_checked_at_startup` | Mode defaults (300 s live, 0 mock) and each out-of-range value as one error naming its variable |
| `test_bad_retry_setting_stops_the_gateway` | Unparsable and negative values stop a `Runtime` with one `ConfigError` naming both |

### `tests/test_offpath.py`

Off-path quality checks (FR-12.31, NFR-1, NFR-3, INV-15; [ADR 0072](adr/0072-off-path-audits-and-shadow.md)): the sidecar, `Ledger.update_request` and the pending rows the gate ignores, and the router's audit and shadow after the response. Threads meet at known points through events (a `GatedProvider` teacher that answers only once released and says when a call started; a gated `FakeOpenAIServer` for the gateway), and every wait is bounded (`WAIT_S`), never a guess; `SideCar.drain` waits for the jobs. Metrics are process-wide, so the tests compare deltas.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_sidecar_without_workers_runs_nothing` | `SideCar(0)` takes no places (and does not call itself full), drains and closes at once |
| `test_sidecar_is_bounded_never_blocks_and_keeps_places_for_audits` | Refusals return at once; shadow work stops before the audits' places, an audit takes the last one; a cancelled place is free again; a slot ends once |
| `test_sidecar_audit_places_scale_with_the_queue` | `audit_places` for queues of 1, 2, 10 and 1000 places and `reserve=0`; bad arguments |
| `test_sidecar_bounds_the_request_text_its_jobs_hold` | `max_bytes` with its share kept for audits: a reservation past it is refused (and counted as full), a job larger than the whole budget is never taken, a running job holds its bytes, and a cancelled, finished, abandoned or refused job gives them back; `job_bytes` counts wide characters' storage |
| `test_sidecar_jobs_run_in_the_submitting_context_and_failures_are_counted` | A job sees the submitter's context variables; a job that raises is counted and logged by its class, never its message |
| `test_sidecar_close_drains_then_gives_up_and_refuses_late_jobs` | `close` waits, gives up on a queued job (returned by tag), refuses a job handed over after it, lets a running job finish; a second `close` gives up on nothing more |
| `test_update_request_writes_each_verdict_once_and_adds_costs_call_by_call` | A verdict written only while pending, `stored` only raised, costs summed in order to the same float as Python's, a missing row, refused columns and values that write nothing |
| `test_update_request_does_not_invalidate_the_hot_path_caches` | Finishing a row leaves the `requests` generation alone; any other rewrite moves it |
| `test_the_ledger_connection_is_used_only_under_its_lock_once_a_sidecar_took_a_job` | FR-12.31: `LedgerGuard` (tests/conftest.py): no sidecar job, any use is fine; once one was queued, `ledger.db.execute` without `ledger.locked()` is refused with how to fix it (the writer connection's Python callback against the GIL deadlocks otherwise), under the lock or after `drain` it is not |
| `test_live_quality_ignores_pending_audits_until_their_verdict` | Pending audits count in neither number; a verdict counts once written, a failed audit is forgotten; the day's audit agreement; a fresh tally reads the same |
| `test_shadow_quality_ignores_pending_shadow_rows` | A pending shadow row counts once its verdict is written |
| `test_an_audited_answer_returns_before_the_teachers_audit_ends` | With the teacher blocked, the answer returns with `overhead_pending`, the serving cost and no audit in the breakdown, the row pending; once released, the row has the verdict, the audit's cost and baseline, `stored`, and the gate counts it |
| `test_offpath_checks_leave_the_rows_an_inline_run_writes` | 60 scripted requests inline and off the path (audits that agree, disagree, fail or measure nothing; shadows that agree, disagree and are arbitrated, fail to sample or meet a failed audit; escalations): every request row (costs to the last bit), example, failure and event, the failure counters, `version_live_quality` and `shadow_quality` are equal, and so is what `ratchet_cost_usd_total` and `ratchet_baseline_usd_total` count (once per cent: the ledger's totals) |
| `test_a_drawn_audit_runs_inline_when_the_queue_is_full` | With every place held, the drawn audit runs before the response, counted per version, with a `sidecar_inline` event |
| `test_a_long_request_past_the_byte_budget_audits_on_the_request_path` | A request whose text would pass the byte budget audits before the response; a short one still goes off the path; both rows end audited |
| `test_a_shadow_queued_after_an_inline_audit_keeps_the_audit_as_its_reference` | The audit runs inline (queue full), a place frees up and the shadow goes off the path: judged against the counted audit's answer without an arbitration, and after a failed audit not arbitrated (INV-15); one teacher call either way |
| `test_a_shadow_without_a_place_is_skipped_and_counted_but_rides_with_an_audit` | A shadow without a place leaves no shadow record, counted per version with a `sidecar_dropped` event; with an audit drawn it rides in the audit's reserved place and gets its verdict |
| `test_offpath_failures_follow_the_optional_call_rules` | A failed off-path audit: `audited` 0, no arbitration, the disagreement unarbitrated, `audit_errors`, an `audit_error` event and a failures row naming the request; a failed shadow sample: no shadow record, through the shadow's breaker |
| `test_an_offpath_arbitration_gives_up_sooner_than_a_served_answer` | The off-path arbitration passes `deadline_s` 60; the inline one passes none |
| `test_a_provider_deadline_shortens_its_retries_never_lengthens_them` | `OpenAICompatProvider.complete(deadline_s=)` on a fake clock: four attempts by default, two within 3 s, four with a longer deadline |
| `test_requests_without_meta_keep_their_checks_on_the_request_path` | No meta (Scout, the simulator) or `offpath=False`: the checks run inline and `cost_usd` is the row's |
| `test_a_ledger_error_gives_the_place_back` | A failing `log_request` cancels the reserved place |
| `test_a_sidecar_that_stops_meanwhile_hands_the_job_back_to_the_request` | A job refused after shutdown began runs before `handle` returns |
| `test_close_gives_up_on_queued_jobs_and_counts_them_per_version` | Jobs given up at shutdown: counted per version (audits and shadows), one `sidecar_dropped` event, rows left pending; the running job finishes; afterwards checks run inline and a closed sidecar is not counted as full |
| `test_concurrent_requests_all_finish_their_checks` | Eight threads, 120 requests, four workers: every job done, no row pending, costs split consistently, the gate's count equal to the audited rows |
| `test_sidecar_settings_defaults_and_bounds` | Mode defaults (4 live, 0 mock), every out-of-range value an error naming its variable (also through `Settings.validate`), an unparsable one collected by `load` |
| `test_mock_mode_and_the_simulator_keep_every_check_on_the_request_path` | A mock-mode `Ratchet` has no workers; a live one has four, starts no thread before its first job, and keeps calls without meta inline |
| `test_the_gateway_answers_first_and_its_shutdown_drains_the_queue` | A live gateway with a blocked teacher answers with `ratchet.overhead_pending`; `/ratchet/stats` `sidecar` and the `/metrics` gauge; leaving the `TestClient` runs the lifespan, whose drain completes the audit; the `chat` log line has `overhead_pending` and its cost plus the `offpath` line's is the row's |
| `test_a_shutdown_past_the_drain_leaves_rows_pending_and_says_so` | `RATCHET_SIDECAR_DRAIN_S=0`: the queued job is given up with a `sidecar_dropped` event, its row stays pending and uncounted; the running one finishes |
| `test_mock_gateway_answers_have_no_pending_overhead` | Mock mode: no `overhead_pending`, an idle `sidecar` block |
| `test_a_demo_reset_stops_the_old_sidecar` | `Runtime.reset` closes the old cascade's sidecar and builds a new one |
| `test_offpath_job_logs_one_line_without_content` | One `offpath` log line naming the row, its verdict and the cost it added, never the request's text; `ratchet_sidecar_jobs_total{outcome="done"}` |

### `tests/test_limits.py`

Spend and rate limits ([ADR 0063](adr/0063-spend-and-rate-limits.md), FR-12.2, FR-12.3, NFR-3, NFR-5, INV-11, INV-13). Gateways run as live gateways through `Runtime(transports=)` with a `FakeOpenAIServer` teacher whose answers cost a known amount (10 prompt and 5 completion tokens at the teacher's price; cold start, so the teacher answers every request), or in mock mode; the spend meter runs on its own with an injected wall clock (`FakeClock`), the rate limits on the runtime's monotonic `FakeClock`. Concurrency tests block a teacher on a `threading.Event` and send the second request from the main thread while the first waits in a background thread, both through one `TestClient`; the stalled-body test calls the ASGI app directly under `anyio.run`, with a `receive` that waits on an event between body chunks. Live `Settings` pass every limit explicitly (`_live`), because live mode's per-IP default would refuse a burst from one test address.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_key_daily_usd_cap_refuses_once_spent_and_other_keys_go_on` | A cap of 2.5 requests' worth: three 200s with `X-Ratchet-Spend-Today-USD` and `X-Ratchet-Budget-Remaining-USD` counting, the third overshooting by its own cost; the fourth a 429 `insufficient_quota` / `budget_exceeded` with remaining 0, `Retry-After`, `X-Ratchet-Budget-Reset`, `X-Should-Retry: false`, a `budget` failures row and a `budget` rejection; another key without a cap gets a 200 and no spend headers; no requests row for the refusal |
| `test_monthly_cap_resets_on_the_first_and_names_the_later_reset` | On Oct 30 a spent daily and monthly cap: `Retry-After` runs to Nov 1 (the later reset); still refused on the 31st, a new day; answered on the 1st |
| `test_spend_survives_a_restart_on_the_same_ledger` | A second `Runtime` on the same file reads today's requests, dollars and failed spend (a `failures` row) per key and for the gateway, and refuses the spent dollar cap and the used request quota |
| `test_failed_requests_spend_counts_against_the_key` | An unsure student, then a teacher 502: the samples' cost counts against the key's dollars (no request counted), at once and after a restart |
| `test_utc_rollover_resets_the_counters_and_a_clock_moved_back_reads_the_ledger` | 23:59 on Oct 31, then 00:01 on Nov 1: day, month and gateway counters start from zero; a new day within the month keeps the month; a clock moved back to Oct 31 reads that day's gateway rows from the ledger, leaving out a row without a gateway request id; `month_bounds` |
| `test_meter_ignores_bad_costs_and_counts_spend_without_a_request` | Negative, NaN and infinite costs count as 0 (the requests still count); `add(requests=0)` adds dollars only |
| `test_budget_alerts_fire_once_per_share_also_after_a_restart` | 50, 80 and 100% of a key's $5.00 daily cap and 50% of the gateway's $10.00, each once and in order (two shares at once when one request crosses both); a restarted meter on the same ledger repeats none; one started with a lower gateway budget writes the three shares it already passed; the next UTC day starts over; `money` |
| `test_alerts_name_a_tiny_cap_exactly_and_read_it_back` | `money` writes caps of $0.0000001 and $0.0000375 with every digit (never `$0.` or an exponent); a restarted meter reads those alerts back and writes none again |
| `test_an_alert_that_cannot_be_written_is_tried_again` | A failing event write is retried on the next add, never raised |
| `test_quota_path_issues_no_count_query` | With a sqlite trace callback: `SpendMeter.usage` issues no statement, and a chat request under a daily quota and dollar budget issues no `COUNT(` of any kind |
| `test_student_only_serves_a_confident_student_and_refuses_what_needs_the_teacher` | The first request's audit spends the gateway's budget; then a confident student answer is a 200 with no audit and no shadow (route `student`, `audited` 0, no shadow version, one student call); an escalation is a 429 `gateway_budget_exceeded` that names no amount, with one `budget` failures row carrying the samples' cost; a pinned task and a request with `tools` get the 429 without any model call; a `budget` event at 100% |
| `test_block_mode_refuses_every_chat_request_until_midnight` | Past the gateway's budget every key's chat request is a 429 `gateway_budget_exceeded`; `/v1/models` still answers; `/ratchet/stats` `budget` block |
| `test_a_scrubbed_public_read_names_no_key_and_no_cap` | A public live dashboard read anonymously after a key's cap and the gateway's budget were reached: every `budget` event reads `client-1 reached N% of its daily budget` or `the gateway reached ...` with no amount, no key name or `$` anywhere, `daily_cap_usd`, `budget.daily_usd` and `remaining_usd` null; a dashboard key reads the events and caps as written; `public_alert` on a detail that is not an alert and on a name that is no longer a key |
| `test_scout_spend_stays_under_its_own_guard` | A Scout run spends money that neither the meter nor a fresh one counts; `/v1` spends the budget, the next chat request is refused, Scout still runs |
| `test_key_limits_override_the_defaults_and_bad_specs_stop_startup` | A per-key `daily_requests` override against the default; `parse_key_limits` with spaces and every field; 22 malformed specs, each naming `RATCHET_KEY_LIMITS` and never echoing a pasted key (`acme:<key>` in `RATCHET_API_KEYS`' syntax, a key as a field, a value or a task list), and a 400-digit dollar cap that would read as infinite; a `Runtime` naming an unknown key does not start |
| `test_task_scope_is_403_and_lists_only_the_scoped_tasks` | A pinned task and `default` outside the scope are 403 `task_not_allowed`; an unknown-task 400 lists only the scope's tasks; the refusals used no quota; another key sends the pinned task |
| `test_store_zero_keeps_a_keys_requests_out_of_the_training_set` | `store=0`: the key's teacher answer is served with `stored` 0; another key's is stored |
| `test_rpm_bucket_refuses_past_the_burst_and_refills_on_the_clock` | 60 a minute: ten 200s with the remaining count falling to 0; the 11th a 429 `rate_limit_exceeded` with `Retry-After: 1`, reset `10s`, no `X-Should-Retry`, a `rate` row and rejection; another key unaffected and without rate headers; one more a second later; the burst back a minute later |
| `test_daily_quota_headers_win_over_the_rate_headers` | A key with both: the 200's `X-RateLimit-*-Requests` describe the daily quota |
| `test_concurrency_one_refuses_a_second_request_while_the_first_runs` | `concurrency=1`: while the first request waits on the teacher, the key's second is a 429 with `Retry-After: 1`; another key is answered; after the first ends the slot is free again |
| `test_max_inflight_answers_503_instead_of_queueing` | `RATCHET_MAX_INFLIGHT=1`: a second request is a 503 `overloaded` with `Retry-After: 1`, an `overloaded` rejection and a `rate` row; the in-flight count returns to 0 |
| `test_a_stalled_body_holds_no_in_flight_slot` | Straight into the ASGI app with `RATCHET_MAX_INFLIGHT=1` and `concurrency=1` for one key: two requests send headers and 5 bytes of chunked body, then stall; the in-flight counts stay 0 and a whole request from each key is a 200; the stalled bodies, released one at a time, are 200s too (the code before the fix refused the second stalled request at once) |
| `test_per_ip_rate_on_a_gateway_without_keys` | Live default 120, mock 0; an open gateway with 6 a minute: the second request from one address (the first `X-Forwarded-For` hop) is a 429 with `Retry-After: 10` that does not echo the address; another address is answered; ten seconds later the first is again; with API keys the per-IP rate is off |
| `test_token_bucket_concurrency_and_limiter_units` | `TokenBucket` (burst, refill, a clock moved back), `burst`, `RateLimiter`'s LRU bound and a new bucket for a new rate, `Concurrency`, `Limiter.rate` (takes a token, holds no slot; refused when empty) and `Limiter.hold` (the in-flight cap, then the key's; a refusal gives back the slot it took) |
| `test_limit_settings_are_checked_at_startup` | `limit_policy` reads the mode in any case and the shares sorted, once; thirteen bad values (a dollar default past a billion and a NaN gateway budget among them) each name their variable |
| `test_stats_report_each_keys_spend_and_cap_and_the_security_switches` | `clients[].spent_today_usd` and `daily_cap_usd`, the three `security` keys and the `budget` block; key names only, never keys |
| `test_router_refuses_teacher_work_under_student_only_without_a_gateway` | `RequestMeta(student_only=True)` on `Ratchet` itself: a pinned task raises `BudgetExceeded` (`pinned`) before any call and writes nothing; an unsure student raises it (`escalation`) after its samples with a `budget` row (429, the samples' cost) |
| `test_daily_quota_holds_room_for_requests_in_flight` | FR-12.2, NFR-5: a quota of 3 with three requests held inside the teacher (none counted yet, `meter.held` 3): a fourth is 429 `daily_quota_exceeded` at once, another key still answers; when the three finish the count is 3, nothing is held and the next is refused |
| `test_a_failed_request_gives_its_quota_room_back` | FR-12.2: a teacher error leaves nothing held and nothing counted, so a quota of 1 still admits the next request |

### `tests/test_admin.py`

The admin API and CLI and compare-and-set release moves ([ADR 0064](adr/0064-admin-api-compare-and-set-and-audit-trail.md), FR-12.1, FR-12.9, INV-5, INV-8, INV-10, INV-13). HTTP tests run live gateways through `Runtime(transports=)` with `FakeOpenAIServer` teacher and student; the races run two `Ledger` instances on one file, as the gateway's tick and the cron tick do, and make the other process act between one gate's reads and its write (`_first_call_runs`), then repeat the race with real threads.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_transition_moves_a_version_only_from_where_the_caller_saw_it` | A stale share or state moves nothing and writes no event; the right one moves, writes its event and starts a new stage; the same decision twice is refused; a move into shadow or canary while another version is in flight is refused; a NULL share reads as 0; a move to live, an unknown state and a share past 100 raise |
| `test_promote_live_retires_the_old_live_version_in_the_same_transaction` | A caller that saw no live version promotes nothing; the promotion retires the old live version, `live_retired` before `live`; the same promotion again writes nothing; promoting a live version raises |
| `test_a_transaction_commits_its_moves_and_events_together_or_not_at_all` | An exception inside rolls back the move and its event, the listener hears nothing and the routing snapshot built inside is dropped; nested transactions join; listeners hear events after the commit; a writer that commits by itself inside raises |
| `test_two_ticks_on_one_ledger_file_advance_a_canary_once` | Gate B has read a ready canary when gate A advances it: one `canary 50%` event, 50% on both connections; six threaded races, one advance each |
| `test_a_promotion_race_leaves_exactly_one_live_version` | Two gates promoting one canary: one live version, one `live` and one `live_retired` event; an admin (CLI, another connection) promoting while the gate is about to: one live version, no gate events, one `admin_actions` row |
| `test_admit_leaves_a_version_an_admin_moved_alone` | A candidate an admin promoted to canary while it was scored stays a canary, with a `conflict` event |
| `test_every_admin_route_needs_an_admin_key` | All 16 `/ratchet/admin/*` routes have the `admin` level; no key 401, an API key and a dashboard key 403, nothing changed; live mode without admin keys 403 `admin API disabled` on each; an admin key does not open the mock-only test controls (INV-8); no key in the ledger (INV-13) |
| `test_canary_rollback_over_http_writes_the_move_an_audit_row_and_an_event` | The answer's before and after; the canary `rolled_back`, live untouched; the `admin_actions` row (actor `api:ops`, reason, before, after, the response's `x-request-id`); the `admin` event; a second rollback 409 and nothing written; the audit log and its `limit` bounds |
| `test_promote_to_live_over_http_retires_the_old_live_version_atomically` | The next canary stage by default, an unchanged share answered `changed: false` with no row, then live with both versions in before and after and exactly one live; the event; `GET /ratchet/admin/versions` with golden numbers and stage audits |
| `test_refused_promotions` (13 cases) | A rejected version to live or canary without force, a rolled-back version or a candidate without force, live to canary, shadow to live (409); a share of 100 or a string, a share for live, an unknown `to`, a non-boolean `force` or `load_adapter`, an unknown key (400); nothing changed or recorded |
| `test_the_transition_table` | Forced returns of a rejected, rolled-back, retired and candidate version to canary, refused while another is in flight; no eval, never; shadow to canary by default 10%, then 50%, then the last stage refused; rollback of live refused with a hint; retiring live; a retired version cannot be retired; 404 and 400 for a bad id |
| `test_a_reason_is_required_checked_and_redacted` | Missing, short, blank, long, two-line and non-string reasons and malformed actors (one that looks like a key) refused with nothing changed; an e-mail address in a reason stored as `[EMAIL]`, nowhere in the ledger |
| `test_load_adapter_before_a_live_promotion` | A failing `/v1/load_lora_adapter` is a 502 naming the class and status, nothing changed or recorded; a working one is called with the adapter's name, path and the endpoint key before the move; 409 without a student endpoint; ignored in mock mode |
| `test_student_disabled_sends_every_request_to_the_teacher_and_stores_as_usual` | With a live version and a shadow: route `student_disabled`, no student or shadow resolution, the teacher's answer stored, no version on the row; switched back on, the student serves |
| `test_student_kill_switch_over_http` | `disable` answers before and after, the next request is the teacher's with no student call, a second `disable` writes nothing, `controls` and the by-task routes in `/ratchet/stats`, `enable`, `GET /ratchet/admin/flags` with who set it |
| `test_a_runtime_pin_keeps_a_task_on_the_teacher_and_out_of_training` | A pinned task (by name and as a `Task` object) takes route `pinned` with no student call and nothing stored (INV-10); its stored rows leave the training set (`excluded_pinned`); its `spec_hash` is unchanged; a spec-pinned task's pin is a no-op and its unpin 409; unknown and invalid names; unpinned, the registered task is back and so are its rows |
| `test_task_pins_over_http_show_in_stats_and_the_task_list` | `POST .../pin` sends the next request to the teacher; `controls.pinned_tasks`; `runtime_pinned` true with the spec's `pinned` false; 404 for an unregistered name; `DELETE` restores the student |
| `test_put_audit_rate_changes_behaviour_without_a_restart` | Rate 0, then 1 (every answer audited), then null (the configured rate again); four bad values 400; `audit_rate` required; 0 during a canary 409, accepted with `force`; with audits off a new canary needs force |
| `test_training_paused_skips_the_night_before_anything_else` | A paused night trains and evaluates nothing (reason `training paused`, a `skip` event) though there is plenty of data; resumed, it trains |
| `test_frozen_releases_hold_every_promotion_but_never_a_rollback_or_an_alert` | Frozen: a cold start shadows the teacher, a ready shadow is held, then promoted on its evidence once unfrozen; a good canary does not advance; a bad canary is rolled back and the live alert fires while frozen; an audit rate of 0 holds a ready shadow too |
| `test_a_frozen_gate_in_a_corrupt_deploy_simulation` | `sim.simulate.run` on a ledger file, freezing from another connection when v1 becomes a canary (simulation): deployed corrupted, v1 is still rolled back and nothing is promoted after the freeze; deployed sound, it stays at 10% instead of advancing, and the nights skip |
| `test_the_tick_endpoint_runs_the_gate_and_records_who_ran_it` | A forced tick rolls a bad canary back and answers before, after and `moved`; the `rollback` then the `admin` event; a tick without a body; both recorded, the second without a reason |
| `test_cli_changes_reach_a_running_ledger_through_data_version` | On one file, a running ledger with cached switches and routing sees the CLI's `flag`, `rollback` and `pin` at once; `audit-log --json` with actor `cli:<user>` and no request id; `versions` output; exit 1 for a refused move, 2 for bad values (2, nan, a short reason); `default`; a missing ledger is refused and not created |
| `test_bad_flag_rows_written_by_hand_never_fail_a_request` | Unparseable and unknown `runtime_flags` rows read as the defaults; `set_flag` refuses an unknown name and NaN; `admin_actions` refuses a boolean limit |
| `test_requests_from_many_threads_while_an_operator_flips_switches` | Four threads, 100 requests, while the kill switch and a pin flip ten times: no error, every route `student`, `student_disabled` or `pinned`, 100 rows |
| `test_admin_tables_exist_on_an_old_ledger_file` | An old file gains `admin_actions`, `runtime_flags` and `task_overrides` on open |

### `tests/test_calibration.py`

Per-task calibration when several tasks share one adapter (FR-14.1, [ADR 0040](adr/0040-per-task-calibration.md)). Scripted golden rows set each answer's confidence and correctness exactly; the simulation numbers come from mock models.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_cascade_eval_per_task_thresholds` | FR-14.1, FR-5.2, INV-6: an easy and a noisy task on one adapter. Pooled, the global threshold (0.85) serves the noisy task's wrong answers (97% overall); the records give the easy task 0.85 and the noisy one 0.95 (precision 1.0, coverage 0.85), with their row counts, agreement, spec hashes and `calibrated`. When nothing reaches its target the noisy task gets 1.01; a `policy.target_precision` of 0.8 gives it 0.85 and a new spec hash |
| `test_single_task_eval_identical_to_global` (3 cases) | FR-14.1, FR-5.2, FR-5.3: with one task, its record holds exactly the global threshold, precision, coverage, row counts and agreement, for `min_task_n` 1, 20 and 1000 (`calibrated` true, true, false) |
| `test_task_with_few_calibration_rows_is_never_below_global` | FR-14.1, FR-5.2: with fewer than `min_task_n` calibration rows a task takes the higher of its own threshold and the global one (`calibrated: false`), the global one with no calibration row, 1.01 with no held-out row; enough rows make it its own |
| `test_task_without_test_rows_is_teacher_only` | FR-14.1, INV-6: a task whose one golden row calibrates (none held out) gets 1.01, and so does a task whose references are all invalid; the router escalates it without a student call (route `task_disabled`) while serving the other task |
| `test_router_uses_task_threshold` | FR-14.1, FR-2.3: at confidence 0.9 task A (0.7) is served, B (0.95) escalates and C (no record) uses the global 0.8; without a task the global threshold, for the fallback model `RATCHET_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD`; each task of a version has its own fingerprint |
| `test_disabled_task_skips_the_student` | FR-14.1, FR-2.3, FR-2.12: a task the gate disabled on the serving version goes to the teacher without a student call (route `task_disabled`, no student cost, confidence null); with the teacher down and `RATCHET_ON_TEACHER_FAILURE=serve_student` another task degrades but the disabled one gets the teacher's error; the shadow still runs; the hosted untuned fallback model ignores the records; a failing resolver leaves the task held back |
| `test_stale_spec_hash_threshold_ignored` | FR-14.1, FR-2.3, FR-2.15: a record under an old spec hash gives 1.01 (neither its own nor the global threshold), and `stale_tasks` names it; a current record wins over a stale `spec_hashes` entry |
| `test_versions_without_task_eval_unchanged` | FR-14.1, FR-2.3: a version without records keeps its one threshold for every task; `spec_hashes` alone still sends a changed task to the teacher; the gate checks only the global numbers |
| `test_threshold_reads_malformed_records_safely` | FR-14.1, FR-2.3, INV-7: records that are not mappings are ignored; a non-numeric or NaN threshold, a missing hash or a disabled record give 1.01; a global threshold that is not a number serves nothing instead of failing the request |
| `test_admit_disables_regressed_task_but_ships_version` | FR-14.1, FR-6.1: 48 of 50 right (96%) is above the 95% floor but more than 2 points behind live's 100%: the task is disabled (threshold 1.01, the reason, a `task_disabled` event) and the version shadows; below the floor (70%) it is disabled with nothing live |
| `test_per_task_gate_holds_each_task_to_its_floor` | FR-14.1, FR-6.1: the global gate's point-estimate rule on each task's own rows: 36 of 40, 9 of 10, 18 of 20 and 91 of 100 right all fail a 95% floor next to a task at 100% (a Wilson upper bound passed them all), and a cold start ships the good task alone; exactly 95% passes; a task that answers none of its held-out rows fails as unmeasured |
| `test_admit_reject_mode` | FR-14.1, FR-6.1: with `task_mode="reject"` the same candidate is rejected, naming the task, and nothing is disabled |
| `test_per_task_gate_needs_evidence` | FR-14.1, FR-6.1: no failure under `min_task_test_n` rows, for a dip within the tolerance (49 of 50 against live's 100%), for a task already at 1.01, or against a live record that serves nothing, has another spec or too few rows; 97% passes the 95% floor but not a policy floor of 99% |
| `test_admit_rejects_a_version_that_would_serve_too_little` | FR-14.1, FR-6.1, INV-5: disabling a task that leaves 12.5% of the held-out rows answered, or per-task thresholds that answer none, reject the candidate instead of leaving it in shadow or canary |
| `test_disabled_task_survives_rescore` | FR-14.1, INV-4: the champion re-check overwrites the eval but keeps the disabled tasks disabled, also one the re-score has no record for |
| `test_per_task_live_alert_once` | FR-14.1, FR-6.7, NFR-8: 12 of 20 audits on one task of the live version raise one alert, not repeated by later ticks or a new gate; `has_event` compares a prefix (a `_` in a task name is not a wildcard); the grouped query runs again only after a new audit; the canary gets its own alert |
| `test_per_task_alert_is_written_once_across_processes` | FR-14.1, FR-6.7: two `Ledger` connections on one file; the second checks for the alert while the first is writing it and waits on the write lock, then finds it: one alert, never two (`Ledger.event_once`: `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, a check and the insert, `COMMIT`); another task or kind is another event; a detail outside its prefix is refused |
| `test_per_task_alert_leaves_single_task_versions_to_the_version_alert` | FR-14.1, FR-6.7: a version whose audits are all one task's gets only the version-level alert |
| `test_stats_by_task_has_audit_agreement` | FR-14.1, FR-7.7: `audits` and `audit_agreement` per task; `threshold`, `precision`, `coverage`, `calibrated` and `disabled` from the live version's records (the global threshold for a task without one, 1.01 for a disabled task or a changed spec, null for a pinned task or without a live version) |
| `test_bom_eval_carries_task_records` | FR-14.1, INV-14: a night's BOM carries the eval's per-task records, and `spec_hashes` matches them |
| `test_task_calibration_settings` | FR-14.1: `RATCHET_MIN_TASK_GOLDEN` (a whole number, 1 or more, reaching `LiveEvaluator`), `RATCHET_TASK_MODE` (case-insensitive, empty is the default, a bad value names the variable) and `RATCHET_MIN_TASK_TEST_N` |
| `test_per_task_calibration_can_be_switched_off` | FR-14.1: `RATCHET_PER_TASK_CALIBRATION=0` makes `cascade_eval` write no records and leaves the pooled numbers as they are; the simulator, the mock gateway and `python -m ratchet.live night` pass it on; only `0` switches it off |
| `test_ledger_merges_a_task_record_atomically` | FR-14.1: `merge_task_eval` merges into one record, keeps the others, and rolls back on an unknown version |
| `test_mixed_workload_calibrates_each_task` | FR-14.1, FR-9.5, US-4: a 6-day mixed simulation (mock models) with `RATCHET_PER_TASK_CALIBRATION=1`: every trained version has a record for `ticket-triage`, `source-triage` and `page-read` under their current specs, and the student still takes over (over 80% on the last day) at audited agreement of 90% or more |

### `tests/test_mock_world.py`

The generic mock world (FR-9.7, [ADR 0041](adr/0041-generic-mock-world.md)): every request the ticket and research worlds do not know gets a deterministic answer from mock models. Schemas are checked with a small validator in the test file (the subset the mock fills), since `jsonschema` is not a dependency; when it is installed, one test checks the same instances with it too. The simulation numbers are trends from mock models.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_lookup_tries_tickets_then_research_then_the_generic_world` | FR-9.7: a test run ticket keeps its world whatever it is sent with (a schema too), a research request as well; `hello`, a system-only request, an image-only request and an empty list get a generic item that answers, and each raises `KeyError` under `strict` (RATCHET_MOCK_STRICT); a schema or tools pick the `schema` or `tools` sub-world |
| `test_generic_items_are_deterministic_and_keyed_by_what_a_model_sees` | FR-9.7: the same request gives the same template, labels and answer whatever task it is sent under; another system prompt gives another template; 60 texts spread over all four framings |
| `test_free_text_world_restates_the_prompt` | FR-9.7: `[mock teacher] You asked: "hello". ...`; a wrong framing differs; a long prompt quotes 12 words and `...`; a 1,000,000-character prompt in under 0.5 s; text parts joined; json_object gives `{"answer": ...}` |
| `test_instance_from_schema_fills_the_structured_outputs_subset` | FR-9.7: 200 seeds of a schema with an enum, a boolean, bounded and exclusive (draft 4 and draft 6) integers and numbers, `multipleOf`, formats (date, date-time, UUID), min and max lengths, a nullable type, `anyOf` with null, `const`, arrays with prefixItems, minItems and uniqueItems, `allOf`, local `$ref`: every instance validates, the same seed gives the same instance, and enums, booleans and integers are drawn |
| `test_instance_from_schema_ends_and_never_raises_on_hostile_schemas` | FR-9.7: a recursive `$ref` ends within `MAX_DEPTH`, a `$ref` loop and 50 × 50 × 50 nested arrays end, bounds of ±1.7e308 and 10^400 give finite JSON, all in under 1 s; 1,500 seeded junk schemas, also as a json_schema and as tool parameters (with `tool_choice` required), never raise, and every label space keeps a wrong value to draw |
| `test_instance_from_schema_agrees_with_jsonschema` | FR-9.7: with `jsonschema` installed (skipped otherwise), the subset and the workload schemas validate under Draft 2020-12 for 100 seeds each |
| `test_schema_answers_stay_valid_for_any_labels_and_wrong_ones_change_enums_first` | FR-9.7: 300 schema-workload items: the teacher's answer and a guessing student's both validate; labels list enums first; every schema task appears; a corrupted deploy's three samples agree on enum values that are all wrong; the mock teacher writes the item's answer |
| `test_mock_teacher_calls_the_catalogue_with_arguments_read_from_the_prompt` | FR-9.7: `lookup_order` with `order_id` 48213, no content, finish `tool_calls`, a stable `call_` id; `refund` with the amount and a reason for each wording (`damaged`, `duplicate_charge`, `not_received`, `wrong_item`, `changed_mind`, a `$` amount too); a plain reply without an order number; after the tool's result a final text answer restating it (inside `{"answer": ...}` for json_object) |
| `test_tool_choice_and_client_tools` | FR-9.7: a forced `tool_choice` wins, `none` gives free text, `required` calls a tool even without an order number; a client's own tool gets arguments that validate against its schema; an integer `order_id` gets an integer |
| `test_mock_student_tool_calls_improve_with_training` | FR-9.7, FR-9.2: on 300 tools-workload requests a student trained on 200 examples of the template makes the teacher's call (name and arguments) over 75% of the time, an untrained one under 60%; a tool-call sample lists no content tokens |
| `test_mock_fleet_trains_and_evaluates_on_generic_and_ticket_rows` | FR-9.7, FR-9.3: a ticket row, a free-text row, a schema row and a tool row with their stored conditions, a system-only row and a content-parts row train without raising (also with `strict`), every template is counted and the golden eval scores all six; a strict fleet's live student raises `KeyError` for `hello` and still answers a ticket; a lenient shadow student answers `hello` on staging |
| `test_strict_teacher_and_unreadable_scout_writer_requests` | FR-9.7: a Scout writer request without readable facts gets a generic answer, and `KeyError` from the strict teacher, as `hello` does |
| `test_mock_strict_setting` | FR-9.7: `RATCHET_MOCK_STRICT` `1` and `0` |
| `test_schema_workload_student_takes_over` | FR-9.7, FR-9.4: `--workload schema`, 4 days × 300 (seed 7, 5 audits and 40 shadow answers to move a release): no student on day 1, over 50% on day 4 and above day 2, audited agreement of 90% or more on the last day; every stored example is one of the three schema tasks and keeps its json_schema condition |
| `test_tools_workload_is_answered_by_the_teacher_alone` | FR-9.7, FR-1.41: `--workload tools`, 3 days × 300: every request goes to the teacher alone (route `tools`, `uses_tools` 1, nothing stored), the student share stays 0 and `stats_by_task` counts 900 `tool_requests` for `order-support` |
| `test_demo_day_runs_the_schema_and_tools_workloads` | FR-9.7, INV-8: `gateway.WORKLOADS` is `sim.world.WORKLOADS`; `/ratchet/demo/day?workload=schema` and `tools` answer 200 with `simulation: true`, their tasks appear in `/ratchet/stats`, and an unknown workload is a 400 |
| `test_sdk_gets_answers_and_structured_outputs_from_the_mock_gateway` | FR-9.7, FR-1.1, US-1: through the OpenAI SDK, `hello` gets a 200 restating it, and `client.chat.completions.parse(response_format=Claim)` (a Pydantic model with a `Literal`, a bounded integer, a date, a nested list and an optional field) returns a parsed `Claim` whose raw content validates against the schema the SDK sent |
| `test_a_mock_tool_call_passes_through` | FR-9.7, FR-1.41: with the default allowlist the mock teacher gets the tools and its call comes back as `message.tool_calls` (null content, `finish_reason: "tool_calls"`), the same call the mock teacher makes directly; with tools kept from the teacher (`RATCHET_TEACHER_PARAMS=max_tokens,stop`) it answers in prose; teacher-only and nothing stored either way |

### `tests/test_structured.py`

Structured outputs (FR-1.40, FR-14.2, [ADR 0042](adr/0042-auto-tasks-from-response-format-schemas.md)): the JSON Schema check (`ratchet/jsonschema_lite.py`), fields derived from a schema and auto tasks (`ratchet/schema_tasks.py`), the contract a `response_format` binds to a request's task, one repair of a teacher answer that breaks it, the json_object fallback, and what clients see. Models are `RecordingProvider`s or `FakeOpenAIServer`s behind `httpx.MockTransport`.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_validator_checks_required_enum_and_additional_properties` | FR-1.40: `required`, `enum` and `additionalProperties: false`, as schema paths that never quote a value or a key from the answer (a secret placed in both is absent); `max_errors`; an `additionalProperties` schema checked under `.*`; a key that is not an identifier written `$["a-b"]` |
| `test_validator_types_and_nullable` | FR-1.40: type lists with null, JSON's integer (`1.0` is one, `true` is not), NaN and infinity refused, an unknown type name ignored, `anyOf` with null |
| `test_validator_resolves_refs_and_guards_against_loops` | FR-1.40: a recursive `$defs` tree, a `$ref` loop that reaches no value, an unresolved or remote `$ref` (an error, never valid), `~1` escapes, `#` |
| `test_validator_combinators_const_items_and_bounds` | FR-1.40: `oneOf` (an integer matches both `integer` and `number`), `allOf`, `const` and `enum` with JSON equality, `items`, `minItems`/`maxItems`, `maxLength`, exclusive and inclusive bounds on a 10^400 integer; `pattern`, `format` and `multipleOf` ignored |
| `test_validator_tuples_and_pattern_properties` | FR-1.40: `prefixItems` with and without `items` (also `items: false`), the old list form ignored; `additionalProperties: false` not enforced next to `patternProperties` |
| `test_validator_never_raises_and_is_bounded` | FR-1.40: `MAX_DEPTH` on a recursive schema; an exploding `anyOf` over 200 items stops at `MAX_STEPS` as a mismatch in under 20 times an ordinary 20,000-item check (timed in the same rounds, so a slow or traced run does not fail it); junk schemas never raise |
| `test_json_equal_is_json_equality` | FR-1.40: `1 == 1.0`, `true != 1`, nested values |
| `test_derive_takes_enums_and_ignores_free_text` | FR-14.2, FR-2.14: the spec's example gives `category` and `priority` labels and ignores `reply`; required follows the schema; no object root, or nothing comparable, gives no fields |
| `test_derive_resolves_refs_nullable_nested_records_and_formats` | FR-14.2, FR-2.14: a Pydantic-shaped schema: `bool`, nullable `date`, `date-time`, exact `text` for `email`, `number` with its relative tolerance, `records` through a `$ref` (free text left out), sets of labels, booleans and dates, a nullable parent's children not required, an integer enum with null; consts, integer lists, bad names and two-way `anyOf` left out; the task accepts what the schema accepts; key order changes nothing |
| `test_an_enum_of_numbers_takes_what_the_schema_takes` | FR-14.2, FR-1.40: an enum of numbers (and an array of one) is an exact `number`, so `2.0` meets the field as it meets the schema; the schema still decides the allowed values; a mixed enum stays a label |
| `test_derive_stops_at_four_levels_and_sixty_four_fields` | FR-14.2: four levels deep, 64 fields wide, a records field counted with its sub-fields |
| `test_x_ratchet_annotations_include_exclude_and_are_stripped` | FR-14.2: `ignore`, `text`, `ordered` (a `ranking`, ordered `records`), `abs_tol`; unknown hints ignored; `strip_annotations` and `forwardable` remove every `x-ratchet` key at any depth but keep a property named that way, and copy nothing when there is none |
| `test_derived_tasks_round_trip_through_a_spec` | FR-14.2: an auto task (source `auto`, never pinned) reads back from `taskspec.document` unchanged, for the test schemas, every mock-world schema and an annotated one; a pinned auto task is refused |
| `test_auto_task_name_is_stable_across_key_order` | FR-14.2: `auto-<slug>-<16 hex>` (at most 62 characters), the same for a reordered schema, another for another schema, 40 slug characters at most, `schema` without a name |
| `test_contracts_and_bound_tasks` | FR-1.40: `contract_for` (cached per schema), `Task.bound` (same `spec_hash`, equal, not registrable), `check` reasons, `clean_answer` serving the JSON inside reasoning, a sentence and a fence, an array root |
| `test_for_row_grades_a_stored_row_under_its_stored_format` | FR-1.40, INV-6: `tasks.for_row` from a dict, JSON text, none and bad JSON, and on a ledger row |
| `test_auto_task_is_registered_persisted_and_never_pinned` | FR-14.2, FR-1.40: the first request registers and stores the auto task (`updated_by` null, the document and hash), one `task_registered` event, the example under its name with the client's format; a second request only looks it up |
| `test_a_registered_task_beats_the_schema` | FR-1.3, FR-14.2: `ticket-triage` named with a schema stays itself, the schema forwarded and still checked (an answer outside it is served with `schema_valid` false and not stored) |
| `test_a_name_the_client_makes_up_becomes_an_auto_task_under_distill_only` | FR-14.2, FR-2.15: an unregistered name becomes an auto task under `distill` (a later schema with other fields gets its own auto task and an `auto_task_collision` event), stays unknown under `teacher` (route `unknown_task`, nothing stored); no name gives the auto task under any policy |
| `test_a_client_cannot_choose_the_fields_another_clients_answers_are_compared_on` | FR-14.2: a client naming another client's computable auto name first with a weaker schema gets its own schema's task; the victim's auto task compares the refund; naming it later with another schema is a collision; a made-up name held by a weaker schema is not used for a stronger one |
| `test_an_auto_name_held_by_other_fields_stores_nothing` | FR-14.2, INV-16: an auto name registered with other fields gives an unregistered task that stores nothing, an `auto_task_collision` event, and a 400 under `reject` |
| `test_a_schema_without_comparable_fields_follows_the_unknown_task_policy` | FR-14.2, FR-2.15: a free-text schema is not registered; `distill` stores it, `teacher` routes it `unknown_task`, `reject` raises `UnknownTask` |
| `test_auto_tasks_stop_at_the_cap_and_say_so_once` | FR-14.2: past `RATCHET_MAX_AUTO_TASKS` new schemas get unregistered tasks; one `auto_task_cap` event, also across two processes on one ledger; 0 turns them off (`default` or the named task, held to the schema, never refused for it); a bad cap stops startup |
| `test_an_auto_task_whose_schema_holds_personal_data_is_not_stored` | FR-14.2, INV-12: an enum of email addresses never reaches the `tasks` table; an `auto_task_skipped` event |
| `test_auto_tasks_come_back_at_startup_and_night_school_grades_under_the_schema` | FR-14.2, INV-6, INV-16: `taskspec.install` registers a stored auto task again; `dataset.rows` and `cascade_eval` hold each row to its stored schema (the student sampled under it), so an answer whose fields agree but which lacks a required free-text key gets no vote and is no training target (`skipped_invalid`) |
| `test_concurrent_requests_register_one_auto_task` | FR-14.2: 8 threads on a new schema register one task, store one row and write one event |
| `test_invalid_student_samples_get_no_vote_and_escalate` | FR-1.40, INV-16: confident samples outside the enum have confidence 0 and escalate; matching ones are served; json_object refuses a non-object |
| `test_json_object_answers_are_served_as_the_json_they_hold` | FR-1.40: json_object serves and stores the object inside reasoning and a sentence, as written; text with no JSON is served as written with `schema_valid` false and not stored |
| `test_an_answer_is_checked_against_its_schema_once_per_request` | FR-1.40: three samples, an audit and the served choice with one text run one schema check (`Contract.problem`'s memo) |
| `test_schema_mismatches_share_one_skip_counter` | FR-1.40, INV-12: schema paths from clients count toward one throttle key; the 1st and 10th events each name their path, never the answer |
| `test_a_repaired_teacher_answer_is_served_and_stored_with_the_original_messages` | FR-1.40, INV-2, INV-16: the repair call carries the request, the first answer without its reasoning and a user turn naming the error; the same parameters; one example with the original messages and the repaired answer; both calls in `cost_usd`, the replaced one in `usage_breakdown.repair`, the baseline one call |
| `test_a_teacher_answer_invalid_twice_is_served_but_never_stored` | FR-1.40, INV-16: served with `schema_valid` false, not stored, a `skip_example` naming the path; a cut-off answer and `n` = 2 are not repaired |
| `test_repair_can_be_switched_off_and_a_failed_repair_serves_the_first_answer` | FR-1.40, INV-15: `RATCHET_SCHEMA_REPAIR=0`; a repair call that raises serves the first answer, counts `repair_errors` and writes `schema_repair_error` with the class only |
| `test_an_audit_answer_is_repaired_too` | FR-1.40: an audit's invalid answer is repaired, counted as an audit and stored |
| `test_arbitration_and_pinned_answers_are_repaired_and_priced` | FR-1.40, INV-10, FR-15.1: an arbitration's repair settles the shadow disagreement and is priced; a pinned task's answer is repaired and priced (baseline one call) and never stored |
| `test_after_a_repair_the_baseline_usage_and_latency_count_the_request_itself` | FR-1.40, FR-15.1, FR-1.12: with a teacher whose prompt tokens grow with what it is sent, the baseline, `usage.prompt_tokens` and `cached_tokens` count the request's own prompt on the escalation, pinned and audit paths; `cost_usd` and the breakdown pay both calls; `latency_ms` adds them |
| `test_a_cut_off_or_empty_repair_answer_keeps_the_first_answer` | FR-1.40, INV-16: a repair answer that was cut off, empty or only reasoning keeps the first answer and its `finish_reason`; nothing stored; the repair call is paid for |
| `test_a_teacher_that_refuses_json_schema_gets_json_object_and_the_schema` | FR-1.40, FR-1.11: a 400 naming `response_format` is retried once as json_object with the schema in the system message (added when there is none); the example keeps the client's format and messages; the schema goes straight to json_object afterwards, one event; another 400 is not retried |
| `test_refused_client_schemas_never_turn_json_schema_off_for_the_others` | FR-1.40, FR-1.11: refused client schemas go as json_object one by one; after `JSON_SCHEMA_STRIKES` the endpoint is probed once with `schema_tasks.PROBE_FORMAT`, takes it, and json_schema stays on for every other schema; no second probe; an inconclusive probe (503) is asked again |
| `test_json_schema_is_turned_off_for_an_endpoint_that_refuses_the_probe_too` | FR-1.40, FR-1.11: an endpoint that refuses every json_schema refuses the probe too: `supports_json_schema` False, every schema as json_object; two events, not repeated by a second process; another endpoint unaffected |
| `test_annotations_never_reach_a_model_or_the_ledger` | FR-14.2, FR-1.13: the teacher's request body and the stored conditions hold the schema without `x-ratchet` keys; the annotation decided the fields; the name hashes the annotated schema |
| `test_fenced_teacher_json_is_served_as_bare_json` | FR-1.40, FR-1.12: a live gateway serves bare JSON from a reasoning-and-fence answer (the SDK's `ChatCompletion` validates it), streamed too, with `task` and `schema_valid` in the `ratchet` block |
| `test_responses_name_the_task_and_stats_name_the_compared_keys` | FR-1.40, FR-14.2, FR-2.15: `ratchet.task` and `schema_valid` for plain, named and schema requests; `compare_keys` and `compare_source` in `/ratchet/stats`; the auto task in `/ratchet/tasks` (`updated_by` null), deletable, a built-in one not; under `reject` a free-text schema is a 400 with `param` `response_format` |
| `test_the_sdk_parse_helper_works_end_to_end_in_mock_mode` | FR-1.40, FR-9.7: `client.chat.completions.parse(response_format=Claim)` against the mock gateway parses into the Pydantic model; the auto task compares `covered`, `decision` and `parts` |
| `test_a_cut_off_answer_is_length_so_the_sdk_raises_its_length_error` | FR-1.40: a cut-off teacher answer is `finish_reason: "length"`, so `.parse()` raises `LengthFinishReasonError`; nothing stored |

### `tests/test_tools.py`

Tool and function calling (FR-1.41, [ADR 0043](adr/0043-tool-calls-teacher-only-by-default.md)): what a tool request is, the legacy fields, the provider reading tool calls, the router answering tool requests with the teacher alone and storing nothing, the response and stream shapes the OpenAI SDK reads, and the multimodal route. Teachers are small providers in the file, `RecordingProvider`s, or `FakeOpenAIServer` / `ToolCallingServer` behind `httpx.MockTransport`; nothing calls a real API.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_uses_tools_names_every_kind_of_tool_request` | FR-1.41: `tools`, `functions`, an assistant `tool_calls` or `function_call`, a `tool` or `function` message, and `tool_choice: "none"` with tools are tool requests; empty `tools` or `functions`, a `tool_choice` or `parallel_tool_calls` with nothing to call, and empty `tool_calls` / null `function_call` are not |
| `test_tool_params_map_the_legacy_fields` | FR-1.41: `functions` become tools, `function_call` the tool choice (`auto`, `none`, `{name}`); legacy only when no current field is sent; a legacy request asks for one call (`parallel_tool_calls: false` unless set); both forms merge (the request's tools first, duplicates by name dropped); nothing about calling is sent without a tool; `GenParams.from_request` carries the mapped fields and never the legacy names |
| `test_legacy_history_pairs_calls_and_results` | FR-1.41: an assistant `function_call` becomes a `tool_calls` entry with a stable `call_` id and the `function` reply a `tool` message with that id; an orphan reply and plain messages stay as they are; the caller's list is untouched; nothing to change returns the same list |
| `test_provider_reads_tool_calls_in_openais_shape` | FR-1.41, FR-1.11: vLLM's call with `index` (dropped, its id kept) and an OpenAI call; object arguments JSON-encoded (non-ASCII kept), missing arguments `{}`, a missing type a function, a missing or empty id `call_` and 24 hex digits (distinct); text beside the calls; a legacy `function_call` answer as one call; `[]` and null as no calls |
| `test_a_tool_call_a_client_could_not_use_is_a_bad_upstream_response` | FR-1.41, FR-1.11, INV-7: 7 cases (not an array, an entry not an object, a `custom` type, no function, no name, a blank name, one bad call of two): each a 502 `bad_upstream_response` |
| `test_tool_call_text_that_is_not_valid_unicode_is_refused` | FR-1.41, FR-1.11: an unpaired surrogate in a name or in the arguments is refused at once, not after the teacher was paid for and the response failed to encode |
| `test_a_tool_request_is_answered_by_the_teacher_alone_and_never_stored` | FR-1.41, INV-2, INV-10: route `tools`, the calls as made, `finish_reason` `tool_calls`, `schema_valid` null; the student never sampled and `add_example` never called; the configured teacher sent the tools, the choice and the parallel flag; `tool_choice: "none"` takes the same path with a prose answer; `n` = 2 choices; `tool_requests` per task |
| `test_tool_history_without_tools_is_teacher_only` | FR-1.41, INV-10: tool calls, a tool result, a legacy call and a `function` message without `tools` each go to the teacher alone with nothing stored; the same task without them is distilled |
| `test_tool_routes_keep_the_operators_reason_first` | FR-1.41, INV-10, FR-2.15: a pinned task and an unregistered one under `teacher` keep their route (tool calls returned); `teacher_only` gives `tools` or the route passed; a task with `tools: distill` is still teacher-only; a pinned, generic or multimodal teacher-only request without tools whose teacher makes a call is a 502 `tool_calls_unsupported`; `tool_requests` per task; without tools the generic path gives `pinned` |
| `test_a_tool_call_is_not_held_to_the_response_format` | FR-1.41, FR-1.40: a tool call with a json_schema is not repaired and `schema_valid` is null; a prose answer to the same request is checked |
| `test_tool_requests_use_the_fallback_teacher_and_map_teacher_failures` | FR-1.41, FR-2.12, INV-1, INV-7: an unavailable teacher's tool request answered by the fallback teacher (route `fallback_teacher`, nothing stored, `uses_tools` 1); without one the error reaches the gateway |
| `test_a_tool_request_gets_no_audit_and_no_shadow` | FR-1.41, INV-2, INV-10: at audit rate 1 with a shadow candidate a tool request makes one teacher call (`audited` 0, no shadow version on the row); the same request without tools runs both |
| `test_usage_counts_a_tool_calls_tokens` | FR-1.41, FR-1.12: with no completion count from the teacher, the estimate includes the calls' JSON |
| `test_redact_messages_covers_tool_arguments_and_results` | FR-1.41, FR-4.7, INV-12: emails in tool call and function call arguments and in tool and function results (strings and text parts) become `[EMAIL]`, five counted; the caller's messages untouched |
| `test_tool_calls_pass_through_the_gateway` | FR-1.41, FR-1.1, INV-1, INV-2, INV-10: a live gateway whose teacher (MockTransport) makes two calls: the SDK's `ChatCompletion` validates the response, the calls and ids are identical, content null, `finish_reason` `tool_calls`; the teacher got the tools, choice and parallel flag under the configured model although the client sent `gpt-4o`; the student endpoint got nothing; nothing stored; route `tools` in `/ratchet/stats`; a teacher 400 about the request is the client's 400 |
| `test_a_malformed_teacher_tool_call_is_a_502_and_nothing_is_stored` | FR-1.41, FR-1.11, INV-7, INV-16: a call without a function name makes the response a 502 `teacher_unavailable` naming `bad_upstream_response`; the student never asked, nothing stored |
| `test_legacy_function_calling_round_trips` | FR-1.41, FR-1.1: `functions` and `function_call` reach the teacher as `tools`, `tool_choice` and `parallel_tool_calls: false`, a legacy history as tool turns; the answer is `message.function_call` with `finish_reason` `function_call`, streamed as one `function_call` delta (each chunk validated); a teacher's second call is dropped in the legacy form; with `tools` too the current form (both calls); a server that answers the old way gets the current form for a current request |
| `test_streamed_tool_calls_rebuild_with_the_sdks_accumulator` | FR-1.41, FR-1.6, FR-1.12: the role chunk, the text beside the calls, one delta per call with the whole arguments, the finish chunk; every chunk validated and `ChatCompletionStreamState` rebuilds the calls of the non-streamed response; the usage chunk equals its usage; calls alone give a null-content role chunk and no content chunk |
| `test_multimodal_requests_are_teacher_only_with_their_own_route` | FR-1.41, FR-1.10, INV-12: with `RATCHET_TEACHER_MODALITIES=text,image_url` an image request skips the student, stores nothing and is logged with route `multimodal` (a pinned task keeps `pinned`); no image bytes anywhere in the ledger dump; an image with tools counts as a tool request |
| `test_the_openai_sdk_runs_a_tool_loop_against_the_mock_gateway` | FR-1.41, FR-9.7, US-1: the real SDK against the mock gateway: `lookup_order` with the order number, the tool result sent back, the final answer restating it, `.stream()` rebuilding the same call (stable mock ids), the legacy `functions` argument getting `function_call`; nothing stored; four `tools` routes (simulation, mock models) |

### `tests/test_routing.py`

Risk-aware routing (FR-2.50, [ADR 0049](adr/0049-risk-aware-routing.md)): a task's `routing` block. Most tests build a `Ratchet` with `RecordingProvider`s and a `moderation` task (`flagged` bool, `severity` label); the critical-class tests script the golden eval's student answers in each row's prompt.

| Test | Covers |
|---|---|
| `test_escalate_when_flagged_forces_teacher` | FR-2.50, FR-2.4: a confident `flagged: true` answer goes to the teacher with route `rule:0`, confidence logged, both costs counted, the teacher's answer stored (`stored` 1); an unflagged one is served by the student; below the threshold the route is `confidence` |
| `test_min_confidence_rule` | FR-2.50: `min_confidence` 0.99 escalates at 0.98 and serves at 0.9996; rules run in order and the first that holds names the route; the label comparator applies (`HIGH` is `high`) |
| `test_rule_answer_never_stands_in_for_the_teacher` | FR-2.50, FR-2.12: with `serve_student` and the teacher down, a flagged answer raises the teacher's error on the rule route and after a low confidence; an unflagged one degrades as before; nothing stored |
| `test_shadow_runs_the_rules_too` | FR-2.50, FR-6.3: a shadow whose answer a rule holds for logs `shadow_serve` 0; with an unflagged answer it serves and agrees |
| `test_long_input_skips_student_and_is_not_stored` | FR-2.50, INV-12: a 101-token prompt over a 50-token limit: route `ineligible:input_tokens`, no student or shadow call, not an escalation, nothing stored and no `skip_example` event; 50 tokens exactly is eligible; with `store_ineligible` the answer is stored (an invalid one is not); `n` 2 gets two choices; a failing teacher raises |
| `test_eligibility_runs_after_pinned_and_before_the_version` | FR-2.50, FR-2.9, FR-14.1: a pinned task's route is `pinned`; an over-length request on a task disabled on the version is `ineligible:input_tokens`, a short one `task_disabled`; with a canary in flight an ineligible request takes no draw from the canary split |
| `test_image_part_skips_student` | FR-2.50, FR-1.10, INV-12: through the gateway with `RATCHET_TEACHER_MODALITIES=text,image_url`, an image request reaches the teacher as sent, never the student, stores nothing even with `store_ineligible`, and logs route `ineligible:content_type`; a text request is served by the student; `/ratchet/stats` shows both routes |
| `test_ineligible_reads_every_message_shape` | FR-2.50: `input_chars` counts content strings, text parts, names, refusals, tool call ids, names and arguments and skips anything else; text parts and an assistant's refusal are text, a user's refusal part or an unknown part is not; the content check comes before the length check; `stores` only for an over-length request with `store_ineligible` |
| `test_task_audit_rate_override` | FR-2.50, FR-2.5: `policy.audit_rate` 1.0 audits every served answer of its task and 0 none, under a global 0.5; another task is audited on exactly the same requests whatever rate a neighbour task sets (one draw either way) |
| `test_route_logged_and_in_stats_by_task` | FR-2.50, FR-2.11: `stats_by_task` counts `rule:0`, `ineligible:input_tokens` and `student`; an ineligible request is not an escalation; `known_route` accepts the fixed routes and the two families and refuses `rule:`, `rule:x`, `rule:-1`, a non-ASCII digit and unknown reasons |
| `test_cascade_eval_measures_critical_misses` | FR-2.50, FR-5.2, INV-6: 2 of 40 flagged held-out rows answered unflagged at the threshold are misses (and right for a second class on `severity`); answered below the threshold, sent to the teacher by a `min_confidence` rule, or refused by the endpoint, they are not; a miss just under the exact calibrated threshold but at the stored (rounded) one counts; an invalid answer is never served; no rows in the class give an upper bound of 1.0 |
| `test_critical_miss_rate_disables_task_at_admit` | FR-2.50, FR-6.1, FR-14.1: a candidate whose pooled numbers pass but which misses 2 of 40 flagged rows (upper bound 16.5% > 10%) has the task disabled with the reason, a `task_disabled` event, and ships for its other task; the router then sends the task to the teacher without a student call; `RATCHET_TASK_MODE=reject` rejects the version |
| `test_critical_gate_needs_enough_rows_in_the_class` | FR-2.50, FR-6.1: with no misses 35 rows pass a 10% limit and 34 fail; 9 rows fail a 50% limit for want of 10 rows and 10 pass; `RATCHET_MIN_TASK_TEST_N` sets the minimum; the limit is the spec's; a record without measurements, with an empty or broken list, under another spec, or with a malformed class entry fails |
| `test_critical_task_without_measurements_stays_on_teacher` | FR-2.50, FR-14.1: without a record, or with one that lacks `critical`, the task is held back (route `task_disabled`, threshold 1.01) while other tasks keep the global threshold; a measured record serves; the hosted untuned model never serves the task and is not sampled for it (route `task_disabled`), while other tasks still sample it; a resolver that fails leaves the request to the student path (`student_error`); a shadow without a measured record does not serve it |
| `test_per_task_off_still_records_critical_tasks` | FR-2.50, FR-14.1: with `per_task=False` only the task with critical classes gets a record, with its measurements; without such a task there is no `tasks` |
